\\\"\\\"\\\"Helper method for :navigate when `where' is increment/decrement.\\n75: \\n76:     Args:\\n77:         url: The current url.\\n78:         count: How much to increment or decrement \\n110: def path_up(url, count):\\n111:     \\\"\\\"\\\"Helper method for :navigate when `where' is up.\\n112: \\n113:     Args:\\n114:         url: The current url.\\n115:         count: The number This should raise an error, not produce a negative value\n        with pytest.raises(navigate.Error):\n            navigate.incdec(base_url, 2, 'decrement')\n\n        # Also test the boundary case: 0 - 1 = -1 should error\n        base_url = QUrl('http://example.com/page_0')\n        with pytest.raises(navigate.Error):\n            navigate.incdec(base_url, 1, 'decrement')\n\n    def test_negative_value_in_result(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        Bug 2: If decrement allows negative values, the result URL would contain \"-\".\n\n        This test catches if the bug allows the actual negative value to be set.\n        \"\"\"\n        base_url = QUrl('http://example.com/page_5')\n\n        try:\n            # Try to decrement by a large amount\n            result = navigate.incdec(base_url, 10, 'decrement')\n            result_str = result.toDisplayString()\n\n            # If the bug exists, the result might be something like \"page_-5\"\n            if 'page_-' in result_str or 'page%-' in result_str:\n                pytest.fail(\n                    f\"Negative value was produced in URL: {result_str}. \"\n                    f\"Should have raised an error instead.\"\n                )\n        except navigate.Error:\n            # Expected behavior - should raise error\n            pass\n\n    def test_encoded_space_in_path(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        Bug 3: URL-encoded spaces (%20) are improperly decoded.\n\n        A URL like /path%20with%20spaces/file5 should keep the %20 encoded\n        when incrementing the number, resulting in /path%20with%20spaces/file6,\n        not /path with spaces/file6.\n        \"\"\"\n        base_url = QUrl('http://localhost/path%20with%20spaces/file5')\n        expected_url = QUrl('http://localhost/path%20with%20spaces/file6')\n\n        try:\n            result = navigate.incdec(base_url, 1, 'increment')\n            result_str = result.toDisplayString()\n            expected_str = expected_url.toDisplayString()\n\n            # Check that %20 is preserved (or properly handled by QUrl)\n            # The display string might decode %20 to space, but the encoded version\n            # should be preserved\n            assert result == expected_url, (\n                f\"Expected {expected_str}, got {result_str}. \"\n                f\"Encoded spaces may have been corrupted.\"\n            )\n        except navigate.Error as e:\n            pytest.fail(f\"Should not raise error for valid increment: {e}\")\n\n    def test_encoded_characters_in_hostname(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        Bug 1: Encoded characters in hostnames with digits.\n\n        Test with %45 (letter E) to ensure encoded sequences in hostnames\n        don't interfere with number matching.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Hostname with encoded char and number\n        base_url = QUrl('http://host%4501.example.com/test')\n        expected_url = QUrl('http://host%4502.example.com/test')\n\n        try:\n            result = navigate.incdec(base_url, 1, 'increment')\n            assert result == expected_url, (\n                f\"Expected {expected_url.toDisplayString()}, \"\n                f\"got {result.toDisplayString()}. \"\n                f\"Encoded sequence in hostname may have been corrupted.\"\n            )\n        except navigate.Error as e:\n            # The %3A should be left alone, and 5 should be incremented to 6\n        base_url = QUrl('http://localhost/%3A5')\n        expected_url = QUrl('http://localhost/%3A6')\n\n        try:\n            result = navigate.incdec(base_url, 1, 'increment')\n\n            # When incrementing a number\n        in a query parameter, only the target number should change, not the\n        percent-encoded sequences.\n        \"\"\"\n        # URL has both encoded sequence and a number to increment\n        base_url = QUrl('http://localhost/search?q=%3Atest&page=5')\n        expected_url = QUrl('http://localhost/search?q=%3Atest&page=6')\n\n        try:\n            result = navigate.incdec(base_url, 1, 'increment')\n            assert result == expected_url, (\n                f\"Expected {expected_url.toDisplayString()}, \"\n                f\"got {result.toDisplayString()}. \"\n                f\"Encoded sequence in query string may have been corrupted.\"\n            )\n        except navigate.Error as e:\n            pytest.fail(f\"Should not raise error for valid increment: {e}\")\n\n    def test_encoded_sequence_in_anchor(self, config_stub_with_incdec):\n        \"\"\"\n        Bug 1: Encoded sequences in anchors are corrupted.\n        \"\"\"\n        base_url = QUrl('http://localhost/page#section%3Atest5')\n        expected_url = QUrl('http://localhost/page#section%3Atest6')\n\n        try:\n            result = navigate.incdec(base_url, 1, 'increment')\n            assert result == expected_url, (\n                f\"Expected {expected_url.toDisplayString()}, \"\n                f\"got {result.toDisplayString()}. \"\n                f\"Encoded sequence in anchor may have been corrupted.\"\n            )\n        except navigate.Error as e:\n            pytest.fail(f\"Should not raise error for valid increment: {e}\")\n\n    def test_negative_value_decrement_below_zero(self, config_stub_with_incdec):\n        \"\"\"\n        Bug 2: Decrementing below zero produces negative values instead of error.\n\n        When decrementing a value by more than it contains, the result should be\n        an error, not a negative number. For example, decrementing 1 by 2 should\n        raise navigate.Error, not produce -1.\n        \"\"\"\n        base_url = QUrl('http://example.com/page_1')\n\n        # Decrement by more than the value (1 - 2 = -1)\n        # This should raise an error, not produce a negative value\n        with pytest.raises(navigate.Error):\n            navigate.incdec(base_url, 2, 'decrement')\n\n        # Also test the boundary case: 0 - 1 = -1 should error\n        base_url = QUrl('http://example.com/page_0')\n        with pytest.raises(navigate.Error):\n            navigate.incdec(base_url, 1, 'decrement')\n\n    def test_negative_value_in_result(self, config_stub_with_incdec):\n        \"\"\"\n        Bug 2: If decrement allows negative values, the result URL would contain \"-\".\n\n        This test catches if the bug allows the actual negative value to be set.\n        \"\"\"\n        base_url = QUrl('http://example.com/page_5')\n\n        try:\n            # Expected behavior - should raise error\n            pass\n\n    def test_encoded_space_in_path(self, config_stub_with_incdec):\n        \"\"\"\n        Bug 3: URL-encoded spaces (%20) are improperly decoded.\n\n        A URL like /path%20with%20spaces/file5 should keep the %20 encoded\n        when incrementing the number, resulting in /path%20with%20spaces/file6,\n        not /path with spaces/file6.\n        \"\"\"\n        base_url = QUrl('http://localhost/path%20with%20spaces/file5')\n        expected_url = QUrl('http://localhost/path%20with%20spaces/file6')\n\n        try:\n            result = navigate.incdec(base_url, 1, 'increment')\n            result_str = result.toDisplayString()\n            expected_str = expected_url.toDisplayString()\n\n            #
